### 111. Holodeck OTC story.txt
- **ID:** `IRDmGOYjVF63IZhBaj3O`
- **Date:** Unknown
- **Description:** From an online role-play server session: 

The subharmonic phase variance of the shuttle's engines nearly drove me out of my head.  I kept wanting to get up, grab a tricorder and an engineering kit and get under the deck to set the EPS flow regulators back to spec.  The inaudible rumble as carrying through the seat and into my spine, setting my teeth on edge.  Still, I knew I'd soon get my chance to fix things like this during the training aboard the station.

![Holodeck OTC story.txt](https://media.gigiwatt.com/file/LifeOS-Media/migrated/media/9MPVGVTxE8dXvkCrl1XrWHQzCl23_IRDmGOYjVF63IZhBaj3O/users/9MPVGVTxE8dXvkCrl1XrWHQzCl23/uploads/1764159337101_Holodeck_OTC_story.txt__url)

- **Martha Carter**: A person with a purple background. She is the mother of Ann Cornett and the wife of Richard "Dick" Carter.
- **Try'n Rest (The Cottage)**: A place with a green background. It's located in Fairfax, Virginia, near the Potomac River and Heathsville.
